It's just... too much right now.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like your thoughts are not your own or are being controlled by something outside of yourself?\n\nPatient: No... I don't think so. I don't really know what you mean by that. They're just... my own thoughts.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any significant changes in your mood or emotional state over the past few months?\n\nPatient: I suppose so... yes. It's just... most mornings, I wake up and there's this... this flat, heavy sadness. It's like... like all the color has drained out of the house before I even make it down to the shop. It's just always there.\n\nDoctor: Do you still hear the woman muttering your name when you're alone in the shop?\n\nPatient: Yes... sometimes. If the shop is quiet. I'll be sitting there and... I hear her muttering my name. It sounds like it's coming from the polishing room. Or... sometimes I'll smell burning metal, even when all the torches are completely off. It... it feels real enough that I usually go back there to check. But there's never anyone there.\n\nDoctor: Is your thinking usually disorganized like this, or are there times when it's more organized and focused?\n\nPatient: I... I don't know. It's just... lately, it's been harder to keep things straight. That's actually why I came in. Someone will come into the shop and ask a simple question, like... when a ring repair will be ready. And I try to answer, but... my mind just sort of drifts. I'll start talking about the repair invoices, and then somehow I'm talking about the soil in my garden, and then the mortgage papers... and I can just see them looking at me, completely lost. I just... I can't seem to hold onto the thread of what I'm supposed to be saying.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced these difficulties with your thoughts and explanations when you've had coffee?\n\nPatient: I don't... I don't really drink coffee. So, no.\n\nDoctor: Do you have any times when your thinking is completely clear and organized for several days in a row?\n\nPatient: Not really. ...Like I just told you, about the customers. When they ask about a ring, and I end up rambling about the garden and the mortgage. It's... it's just been like that most days. I don't really get long stretches where it's completely clear right now."
